GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
No:EP149 SLB 2020 Karnataka Government Secretariat,
M.S. Building
Bangalore, Dated:25-01-2023.

NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Karnataka Secondary Education Examination Board (Scheme
of Evaluation) Rules, 2022, was published as required by sub- section (1) of section 145 of
the Karnataka Education Act, 1983 (Karnataka Act 01 of 1995) in Notification No. EP 149
SLB 2020, dated: 21.10.2022 in part IVA of the Karnataka Gazette dated: 16.11.2022
inviting objections and suggestions from all persons likely to be effected thereby within
fifteen days from the date of its publication in the official Gazette.
And whereas the said Gazette was made available to public on 16.11.2022. And
whereas no objections and suggestions have been received by the Government.
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sections 33 and 34 of the
Karnataka Secondary Education Examination Board Act, 1966 (Karnataka Act 16 of 1966)
read with sub-section (2) of section 22 and sub- section (1) of section 145 of the Karnataka
Education Act, 1983 (Karnataka Act 01 of 1995), the Government of Karnataka hereby make
the following rules. namely:-

RULES
1. Title and Commencement:-(1) These rules may be called the Karnataka
Secondary Education Examination Board (Scheme of Evaluation) (Amendment)
Rules, 2023.

(2) They shall come into force from the date of their final publication in the
official Gazette.

2. Amendment of rule 6.- In the Karnataka Secondary Education
Examination Board (Scheme of Evaluation) Rules, 2017, in rule 6,-

(i) in sub-rule (1), for the words “three subjects”, the words “two
subjects”, shall be substituted; and
(ii) in sub-rule (2), for the words “ten percent”, the words “five
percent”, shall be substituted.

By order and in the name of
Governor of Karnataka,

(V. SREENIVASAMURTHY)
Under Secretary to the Government
(Secondary Education)
Department of School Education and literacy

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 314 PFH 2022 Dated:15-11-2022
regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of A Hosahalli village of
Turuvekere Taluk in Tumkuru District for constituting hamlet/extention called
A Hosahallipalya within the boundary of A Hosahalli revenue village was published
as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of
1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 314 PFH 2022 dated:17-11-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Date:18-11-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Goverenmen of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
wihin the limits of A Hosahalli revenue village, namely:-A Hosahallipalya

SCHEDULE -1
Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area ofA Hosahalli village of Turuvekere Taluk
of Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the
boundary ofA Hosahalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named asA
Hosahallipalya of A Hosahalli village Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkuru District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 92 KAMGRARA 2018,
Dated:28-09-2019 regarding altering the limits of Anekere village of Turuvekere
Taluk and Tumkur District and constituting a new revenue village called
Nayakanaghatta was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land

Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No RD 92
KAMGRARA 2018 Dated:08-11-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:15-11-2018. And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
hereby makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Anekere of Turuvekere Taluk in Tumkur District to form a new revenue
village called as Nayakanaghatta of Turuvekere Taluk Tumkur District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

RD 158 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:27-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 158 PFH 2022,
Dated:10-08-2022 regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of
Anthapura village of Gubbi Taluk in Tumkuru District for constituting
hamlet/extention called Anthapura palya within the boundary of Anthapura revenue
village was published as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 158 PFH 2022
Dated:19-08-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Date:23-08-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
within the limits of Anthapura revenue village, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearig survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Anthapura palya village of Gubbi Taluk
of Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the
boundary of Anthapura Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Anthapura palya of Anthapura village in Gubbi Taluk of Tumkuru District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 920 KAMGRARA 2017,
Dated:22-12-2017 regarding altering the limits of Bukkasagara village of Gubbi
Taluk and Tumakuru District and constituting a new revenue village called
Agrahara was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 920
KAMGRARA 2017 Dated:25-01-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And Whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:01-
02-2018 and Whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
hereby makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village specified in column (2) of the table
below and areas specified in column (3) there of shall be excluded from the limits of
said village of Gubbi Taluk in Tumakuru District to form a new revenue village
called as Agrahara of Gubbi Taluk Tumakuru District. The new survey numbers
which will form part of this new village are as specified in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 116 GLH 2022
Dated:27-06-2022 regarding altering the limits of Changavi village of Gubbi Taluk
and Tumakur District and constituting a new revenue village called Chikkachangavi
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 116 GLH 2022
Dated:06-07-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be

affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:15-
07-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

below and areas specified in column (3) there of shall be excluded from the limits of
said village Changavi of Gubbi Taluk and Tumakur District to form a new revenue
village called Chikkachangavi of Gubbi Taluk in Tumakur district along with
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

of 1964) in the RD 325 PFH 2022 Dated 17-11-2022 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected there by with in thirty days from

village of Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk in Tumkur District to form a new revenue

District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

SCHEDULE-2

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Chikkanayakanahalli taluk in Tumkur district to form a new revenue
village called as Emmekarihatti of Chikkanayakanahalli taluk Tumkur District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARANATAKA
RD 99 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:27-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Where the draft of the Notification No. RD 99 GLH 2022 Dated:17-06-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Dodduru village of Lakshmeshwara Taluk and
Gadag District and constituting a new revenue village called Muniyana nagara was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revanue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No. 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 99 GLH 2022
Dated:08-07-2022 inviting objection or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:01-08-
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Nom, therefore, in exercise of the power conferred by section of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnaraka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Dodduru Village of Lakshmeshwara Taluk in Gadag District to form a new revenue
village called as Muniyana nagara of Lakshmeshwara Taluk and Gadag District
along with the Survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 326 PFH 2022 Dated:15-11-2022
regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of Hindiskere village of
Kibbanahalli hobli Tiptur Taluk in Tumkur District for constituting
hamlet/extension called Seegepalya within the boundary of Hindiskere revenue
village was published as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 326 PFH 2022
Dated: 17-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Dated:18-11-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extension
within the limits of Hindiskere revenue village, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Seegepalya village of Tiptur Taluk of
Tumkur District and convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary

of Hindiskere Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Seegepalya of
Hindiskere village Tiptur Taluk of Tumkur District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 161 PFH 2022 Dated:10-08-2022
regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of Hoysalakatte village of
Chikkanayakanahalli Taluk in Tumkuru District for constituting hamlet/extension
called Thimmappanahatti within the boundary of Hoysalakatte revenue village was
published as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
Act 12 of 1964) RD 161 PFH 2022 dated:17-08-2022 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Dated:18-08-2022 and whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extension
within the limits of Hoysalakatte revenue village, namely

SCHEDULE
Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Hoysalakatte village of
Chikkanayakanahalli taluk of Tumkuru district and convert the same to form
hamlet/extention within the boundary of Hoysalakatte revenue village and same will
herein after be named as Thimmappanahatti of Hoysalakatte village
Chikkanayakanahalli taluk of Tumkuru district.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 141 PFH 2022
Dated:13-10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Indaragi village of Koppal Taluk
and Koppal District and constituting a new revenue village called Indiranagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 141 PFH 2022
Dated:15-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And Whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:07-
11-2022
And Whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
hereby makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Indaragi of Koppal Taluk in Koppal District to form a new revenue village
called as Indiranagar of Koppal Taluk Koppal District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 55 PFH 2021
Dated:19-01-2022 regarding altering the limits of Jalageri village of Tikota Taluk
and Vijayapur District and constituting a new revenue village called Jai
Sevalal Nagar, Dhokalawadi, Shree Satya Sevalal Nagar, Gandhi Nagar, Pawar
Nagar ,Vithalawadi and Adishakti Nagar was published as required by section 6 of
the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the

Karanataka Gazette No. RD.55 PFH 2021 Dated:21-04-2022 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:24-8-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Jalageri of Tikota taluk, Vijayapur District to form a new revenue village
called as Jai Sevalal Nagar of Tikota taluk, Vijayapur District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-204
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 360 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat.
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:27-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 360 PFH 2022
Dated:24-11-2022 regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of
Kodihalli village of Honnavalli hobli Tiptur Taluk in Tumkur District for
constituting hamlet/extention called Kodihalli Bhovi Kalony within the boundary of
Kodihalli revenue village was published as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 360
PFH 2022 dated 25-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons
likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the
Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Date:28-11-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
wihin the limits of Kodihalli revenue village, namely:- Kodihalli bhovi kalony.

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearig survey numbers and extent specified below in colum(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Kodihalli bhovi kalony village of Tiptur
Taluk of Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the
boundary of Kodihalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Kodihalli bhovi kalony of Kodihalli village Tiptur Taluk of Tumkuru District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Where the draft of the Notification No. RD 205 PFH 2022 Dated:13-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Kundralli village of Lakshmeshwara Taluk and
Gadag District and constituting a new revenue village called Umanagara was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No. 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette RD 205 PFH 2022
Dated:15-10-2022 inviting objection or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:31-
10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Nom, therefore, in exercise of the power conferred by section of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnaraka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Kundralli Village of Lakshmeshwara Taluk in Gadag District to form a new
revenue village called as Umanagara of Lakshmeshwara Taluk and Gadag District
along with the Survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-206
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 312 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat.
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date :27-01-2023.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 312 PFH 2022 Dated:15-11-2022
regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of Madihalli village of
Turuvekere Taluk in Tumkuru District for constituting hamlet/extention called
Madihallipalya within the boundary of Madihalli revenue village was published as
required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of
1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 312 PFH 2022, Dated:17-11-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Dated:18-11-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
within the limits of Madihalli revenue village, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Madihalli village of Turuvekere Taluk
of Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the
boundary of Madihalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Madihallipalya of Madihalli village Turuvekere Taluk of Tumkuru District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Where the draft of the Notification RD 108 PFH 2022 Dated:17-06-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Majjura village of Shirahatti Taluk and Gadag
District and constituting a new revenue village called Shivajinagara was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revanue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No. 12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette RD 108 PFH 2022 Dated: 11-07-2022 inviting
objection or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty
days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:27-07-
2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Nom, therefore, in exercise of the power conferred by section of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnaraka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing Survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Majjura Village of Shirahatti Taluk in Gadag District to form a new revenue village
called as Shivajinagara of Shirahatti Taluk and Gadag District along with the
Survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 361 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date :27-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 361 PFH 2022
Dated:24-11-2022 regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of
Masavanagatta village of Nonavinkere hobli Tiptur Taluk in Tumkur District for
constituting hamlet/extention called Kamparahalli and Mudalakoppalu within the
boundary of Masavanagatta revenue village was published as required by section 6 of
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. RD 361 PFH 2022 Dated:25-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions
from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Dated:25-11-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of

Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
within the limits of Masavanagatta revenue village, namely:- Kamparahalli and
Mudalakoppalu

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Kamparahalli village of Tiptur Taluk of
Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the boundary
of Masavanagatta Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Kamparahalli of Masavanagatta village in Tiptur Taluk of Tumkuru District.

PR-211
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 05(4) kamgrara 2021 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:27-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 05(4) kamgrara 2021
dated:08-11-2022 regarding altering the limits of Muthikere village of Arasikere
Taluk and Hassan District and constituting a new revenue village called Matthihalli
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 05(4) kamgrara
2021 dated: 09-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely

to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:09-11-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Matthihalli taluk Arasikere in.District to form a new revenue village called
Muthikere of Arasikere Taluk Hassan district along with survey numbers included in
column (4) thereof.

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 315 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:27-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 315 PFH 2022,
Dated:15-11-2022 regarding altering the limits of N Manchenahalli village of
Turuvekere Taluk and Tumkur District and constituting a new revenue village called
Haridasanahalli was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette no.
RD 315 PFH 2022 Dated 17-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Date:18-11-2022. And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
hereby makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village N Manchenahalli of Turuvekere Taluk in Tumkur District to form a new
revenue village called as Haridasanahalli of Turuvekere Taluk Tumkur District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-214
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 16 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:27-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 16 PFH 2022
Dated:19-03-2022 regarding altering the limits of Toravi village of Tikota Taluk
and Vijayapur District and constituting a new revenue village called Atalatti, Majare
Atalatti, Krishna Nayak Nagar, Kesaral Tanda, Majare Kesaral Tanda ,
Maragamma Devi Nagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No.
RD 16 PFH 2022 Dated:28-03-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:05-08-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Toravi of Tikota taluk ,Vijayapur District to form a new revenue village
called as Atalatti of Tikota taluk, Vijayapur District along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

TABLE-1
Sl. No Old Survey numbers Extent of areas transferred Survey numbers specified
which areas are to new village Atalatti in column (2) which are to
excluded from Toravi be included to form new
village to constituting village Atalatti and new
new Village Atalatti Acre Gunta Anna survey numbers assigned to
them.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 148 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:02-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Bankanakatte of Ajjampura taluk Chikkamagalur District and to constitute a new
revenue village called M.Hosahalli(Menasinakayi Hosahalli)as specified in schedule
along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Chikkamagalur District, Chikkamagalur

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said

village Bankanakatte of Ajjampura taluk in Chikkamagalur District to form a new
revenue village called M.Hosahalli(Menasinakayi Hosahalli) of Ajjampura taluk
Chikkamagalur district along with the survey numbers included in column (4)
thereof.

PR-216

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 149 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:02-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Chikkangala of Kadur taluk Chikkamagalur District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Andenahalli as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkamagalur District, Chikkamagalur

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Chikkangala of Kadur taluk in Chikkamagalur District to form a new
revenue village called Andenahalli of Kadur taluk Chikkamagalur district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-217

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 13 PFH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:02-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Arjunahalli Village of Krishnarajanagara taluk Mysore District and to constitute a
new revenue village called Kalyanapura as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Mysore District, Mysore

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Arjunahalli Village of Krishnarajanagara taluk Mysore District to form a new
revenue village called Kalyanapura of Krishnarajanagara taluk Mysore district
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 12 PFH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru,Date:02-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Mallenahalli of Tarikere taluk Chikkamagalur District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Doddaningenahalli as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkamagalur District, Chikkamagalur

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Mallenahalli of Tarikere taluk in Chikkamagalur District to form a new
revenue village called Doddaningenahalli of Tarikere taluk Chikkamagalur district
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Kalkere Village of Kadur taluk Chikkamagaluru District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Manjunathapura as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will

be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkamagaluru District, Chikkamagaluru.

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column(2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kalkere of Kadur Taluk in Chikkamagaluru District to form a new revenue
village called as Manjunathpura of Kadur taluk and Chikkamagalore district with
the survey numbers included in column(4) thereof.

1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the

village and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative
convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the
limits of Panchehosahalli village of Kadur talluk of Chikkamagaluru District and
to constitute a new revenue village called Gowdayyanakoppalu as specified in
schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkamagaluru District, Chikkamagaluru.

SCHEDULE
Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Gowdayyanakoppalu village Kadur
talluk of Chikkamagaluru district and convert the same to form hamlet extension
within the boundary of Panchehoshalli revenue village and same will here in after be
named as Gowdayyanakoppalu of Panchehosahalli village Kadur talluk,
Chikkamagaluru district.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Ummatturu Village of Hunsur taluk Mysuru District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Pakshirajapura-1 as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Mysuru District, Mysuru

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Ummatturu of Hunsur taluk in Mysuru District to form a new revenue
village called Pakshirajapura-1 of Hunsur taluk Mysuru district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

by makes the following notification, namely:-

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 77 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:02-02-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 77 GLH 2022 Dated: 06-
07-2022 regarding altering the limits of Huligondi village of Kadur Taluk in
Chikkamagaluru District and constituting a new revenue village called Hosuru, was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 77 GLH 2022
Dated:21.07.2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:22-
07-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Huligondi of Kadur Taluk and Chikkamagaluru District to form a new

revenue village called Hosuru, KadurTaluk in Chikkamagaluru district along with
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

which are excluded transferred to new in column (2) which are to
from village be included to form new
Huligondi village to Hosuru, village Hosuru, new
constituting new Acre Gunta Anna survey numbers assigned
village Hosuru, to them

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 71 GLH 2022
Dated:17-06-2022 regarding altering the limits of Jodihochihalli, Nagaralu,
Byaladalu village of Kadur Taluk in Chikkamagaluru District and constituting a
new revenue village called Vaderahalli Thandya, was published as required by
section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in
the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 71 GLH 2022 Dated:11.07.2022 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:12-07-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column(2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Jodihochihalli, Nagaralu, Byaladalu of Kadur Taluk in Chikkamagaluru
District to form a new revenue village called as Vaderahalli Thandya of

Chikkamagaluru taluk and Chikkamagaluru district with the survey numbers
included in column(4) thereof.
